&lt;project_context>
# 项目: trace-verdict-study

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: autodl-rtxpro6000
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh autodl-rtxpro6000  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
在 autodl-rtxpro6000 上执行 SFT 模型评估，获取 RE F1 和 Evi F1 锚点。

## 前置：Kill 残留进程
先 kill PID 12569（占 28GB GPU 的残留进程）：
```bash
kill 12569 2>/dev/null; sleep 2; kill -9 12569 2>/dev/null; echo "killed"
```

## 评估命令
```bash
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base
cd /root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python evaluate.py \
  --model_path ./outputs/sft/merged \
  --eval_file ./data/docred_dev.jsonl \
  --output_file ./outputs/sft/eval_results.json
```

**注意**：evaluate.py 的参数名可能是 `--eval_file` 或 `--dev_file`，先跑 `python evaluate.py --help` 确认参数名。

## 服务器
- 连接: `ssh autodl-rtxpro6000`
- 环境: aml-train 镜像, conda base
- 模型路径: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study/outputs/sft/merged`
- 数据: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study/data/docred_dev.jsonl` (985 samples)

## 产出要求
1. 完整的评估指标：RE F1, Evi F1, Attribution Precision（如果有）
2. 如果脚本报错，给出完整错误信息
3. 评估完成后读取 `./outputs/sft/eval_results.json` 内容并报告

这是 MVP 最关键的数据点——决定后续 kill criteria 阈值。

---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef compute_re_f1(pred_rels: list[dict], gold_rels: list[dict]) -> tuple[float, float, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Relation extraction F1 (exact triple match on head+tail+relation).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    pred_set = {\\n        (r.get(\\\"head\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(), r.get(\\\"tail\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(), = tp / len(gold_set) if gold_set else 0.0\\n    f1 = 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if (prec + rec) > 0 else 0.0\\n    return prec, rec, f1\\n\\n\\ndef compute_evi_f1(\\n    pred_rels: list[dict], {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Evaluation script for ProvUIE.\\n\\nGenerates predictions from a trained model and computes:\\n  - RE F1:  relation extraction F1 (exact triple match)\\n  - Evi F1: evidence sentence F1 (DocRED standard)\\n  - Attribution Precision: fraction of extractions backed by evidence\\n\\nInput:  Model checkpoint + evaluation JSONL.\\nOutput: Formatted report + JSON results file.\\n\\nUsage:\\n    python evaluate.py --model_path ./outputs/grpo/final --eval_file ./data/docred_dev.jsonl\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, HfArgumentParser\\n\\nfrom config import ModelArgs, EvalArgs\\nfrom data_utils import load_grpo_dataset\\nfrom rewards import parse_json_output\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Metrics\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef compute_re_f1(pred_rels: list[dict], gold_rels: list[dict]) -> tuple[float, float, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Relation extraction F1 (exact triple match on head+tail+relation).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    pred_set = {\\n        (r.get(\\\"head\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(), r.get(\\\"tail\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(), r.get(\\\"relation\\\", \\\"\\\").lower())\\n        for r in pred_rels\\n    }\\n    gold_set = {\\n        (r[\\\"head\\\"].lower(), r[\\\"tail\\\"].lower(), r[\\\"relation\\\"].lower())\\n        for r in gold_rels\\n    }\\n    if not pred_set and not gold_set:\\n        return 1.0, 1.0, 1.0\\n\\n    tp = len(pred_set & gold_set)\\n    prec = tp / len(pred_set) if pred_set else 0.0\\n    rec = tp / len(gold_set) if gold_set else 0.0\\n    f1 = 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if (prec + rec) > 0 else 0.0\\n    return prec, rec, f1\\n\\n\\ndef compute_evi_f1(\\n    pred_rels: list[dict], gold_rels: list[dict], sentences: list[str]\\n) -> tuple[float, float, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Evidence sentence F1 (per-relation, sentence-level).\\n\\n    Matches predicted evidence text back to sentence indices for comparison\\n    with gold evidence_sids.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    sent_to_idx: dict[str, int] = {}\\n    for i, s in enumerate(sentences):\\n        sent_to_idx[s.strip().lower()] = i\\n\\n    all_pred: set[tuple] = set()\\n    all_gold: set[tuple] = set()\\n\\n    gold_map: dict[tuple, set[int]] = {}\\n    for r in gold_rels:\\n        key = (r[\\\"head\\\"].lower(), r[\\\"tail\\\"].lower(), r[\\\"relation\\\"].lower())\\n        sids = set(r.get(\\\"evidence_sids\\\", []))\\n        if not sids:\\n            for ev in r.get(\\\"evidence\\\", []):\\n                idx = sent_to_idx.get(ev.strip().lower())\\n                if idx is not None:\\n                    sids.add(idx)\\n        gold_map[key] = sids\\n        for sid in sids:\\n            all_gold.add((key, sid))\\n\\n    for r in pred_rels:\\n        key = (\\n            r.get(\\\"head\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(),\\n            r.get(\\\"tail\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(),\\n            r.get(\\\"relation\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(),\\n        )\\n        for ev in r.get(\\\"evidence\\\", []):\\n            if isinstance(ev, str):\\n                idx = sent_to_idx.get(ev.strip().lower())\\n                if idx is not None:\\n                    all_pred.add((key, idx))\\n\\n    if not all_pred and not all_gold:\\n        return 1.0, 1.0, 1.0\\n\\n    tp = len(all_pred & all_gold)\\n    prec = tp / len(all_pred) if all_pred else 0.0\\n    rec = tp / len(all_gold) if all_gold else 0.0\\n    f1 = 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if (prec + rec) > 0 else 0.0\\n    return prec, rec, f1\\n\\n\\ndef compute_attribution_precision(pred_rels: list[dict]) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Fraction of predicted relations that include non-empty evidence.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not pred_rels:\\n        return 0.0\\n    with_ev = sum(1 for r in pred_rels if r.get(\\\"evidence\\\") and len(r[\\\"evidence\\\"]) > 0)\\n    return with_ev / len(pred_rels)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Main\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = HfArgumentParser((ModelArgs, EvalArgs))\\n    model_args, eval_args = parser.parse_args_into_dataclasses()\\n\\n    # ── Load model ────────────────────────────────────���────────────────\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(eval_args.model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    model_kwargs: dict = {\\\"trust_remote_code\\\": True, \\\"torch_dtype\\\": \\\"auto\\\"}\\n    if model_args.use_flash_attention:\\n        model_kwargs[\\\"attn_implementation\\\"] = \\\"flash_attention_2\\\"\\n\\n    # Handle both full models and PEFT adapters\\n    adapter_config_path = Path(eval_args.model_path) / \\\"adapter_config.json\\\"\\n    if adapter_config_path.exists():\\n        from peft import PeftModel\\n\\n        with open(adapter_config_path) as f:\\n            acfg = json.load(f)\\n        base_name = acfg.get(\\\"base_model_name_or_path\\\", model_args.model_name_or_path)\\n        model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(base_name, **model_kwargs)\\n        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, eval_args.model_path)\\n        model = model.merge_and_unload()\\n    else:\\n        model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(eval_args.model_path, **model_kwargs)\\n\\n    device = \\\"cuda\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\"cpu\\\"\\n    model.to(device).eval()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Evaluation script for ProvUIE.\\n\\nGenerates predictions from a trained model and computes:\\n  - RE F1:  relation extraction F1 (exact triple match)\\n  - Evi F1: evidence sentence F1 (DocRED standard)\\n  - Attribution Precision: fraction of extractions backed by evidence\\n\\nInput:  Model checkpoint + evaluation JSONL.\\nOutput: Formatted report + JSON results file.\\n\\nUsage:\\n    python evaluate.py --model_path ./outputs/grpo/final --eval_file ./data/docred_dev.jsonl\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, HfArgumentParser\\n\\nfrom config import ModelArgs, EvalArgs\\nfrom data_utils import load_grpo_dataset\\nfrom rewards import parse_json_output\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Metrics\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef compute_re_f1(pred_rels: list[dict], gold_rels: list[dict]) -> tuple[float, float, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Relation extraction F1 (exact triple match on head+tail+relation).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    pred_set = {\\n        (r.get(\\\"head\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(), r.get(\\\"tail\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(), r.get(\\\"relation\\\", \\\"\\\").lower())\\n        for r in pred_rels\\n    }\\n    gold_set = {\\n        (r[\\\"head\\\"].lower(), r[\\\"tail\\\"].lower(), r[\\\"relation\\\"].lower())\\n        for r in gold_rels\\n    }\\n    if not pred_set and not gold_set:\\n        return 1.0, 1.0, 1.0\\n\\n    tp = len(pred_set & gold_set)\\n    prec = tp / len(pred_set) if pred_set else 0.0\\n    rec = tp / len(gold_set) if gold_set else 0.0\\n    f1 = 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if (prec + rec) > 0 else 0.0\\n    return prec, rec, f1\\n\\n\\ndef compute_evi_f1(\\n    pred_rels: list[dict], gold_rels: list[dict], sentences: list[str]\\n) -> tuple[float, float, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Evidence sentence F1 (per-relation, sentence-level).\\n\\n    Matches predicted evidence text back to sentence indices for comparison\\n    with gold evidence_sids.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    sent_to_idx: dict[str, int] = {}\\n    for i, s in enumerate(sentences):\\n        sent_to_idx[s.strip().lower()] = i\\n\\n    all_pred: set[tuple] = set()\\n    all_gold: set[tuple] = set()\\n\\n    gold_map: dict[tuple, set[int]] = {}\\n    for r in gold_rels:\\n        key = (r[\\\"head\\\"].lower(), r[\\\"tail\\\"].lower(), r[\\\"relation\\\"].lower())\\n        sids = set(r.get(\\\"evidence_sids\\\", []))\\n        if not sids:\\n            for ev in r.get(\\\"evidence\\\", []):\\n                idx = sent_to_idx.get(ev.strip().lower())\\n                if idx is not None:\\n                    sids.add(idx)\\n        gold_map[key] = sids\\n        for sid in sids:\\n            all_gold.add((key, sid))\\n\\n    for r in pred_rels:\\n        key = (\\n            r.get(\\\"head\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(),\\n            r.get(\\\"tail\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(),\\n            r.get(\\\"relation\\\", \\\"\\\").lower(),\\n        )\\n        for ev in r.get(\\\"evidence\\\", []):\\n            if isinstance(ev, str):\\n                idx = sent_to_idx.get(ev.strip().lower())\\n                if idx is not None:\\n                    all_pred.add((key, idx))\\n\\n    if not all_pred and not all_gold:\\n        return 1.0, 1.0, 1.0\\n\\n    tp = len(all_pred & all_gold)\\n    prec = tp / len(all_pred) if all_pred else 0.0\\n    rec = tp / len(all_gold) if all_gold else 0.0\\n    f1 = 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if (prec + rec) > 0 else 0.0\\n    return prec, rec, f1\\n\\n\\ndef compute_attribution_precision(pred_rels: list[dict]) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Fraction of predicted relations that include non-empty evidence.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not pred_rels:\\n        return 0.0\\n    with_ev = sum(1 for r in pred_rels if r.get(\\\"evidence\\\") and len(r[\\\"evidence\\\"]) > 0)\\n    return with_ev / len(pred_rels)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Main\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = HfArgumentParser((ModelArgs, EvalArgs))\\n    model_args, eval_args = parser.parse_args_into_dataclasses()\\n\\n    # ── Load model ────────────────────────────────────���────────────────\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(eval_args.model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    model_kwargs: dict = {\\\"trust_remote_code\\\": True, \\\"torch_dtype\\\": \\\"auto\\\"}\\n    if model_args.use_flash_attention:\\n        model_kwargs[\\\"attn_implementation\\\"] = \\\"flash_attention_2\\\"\\n\\n    # Handle both full models and PEFT adapters\\n    adapter_config_path = Path(eval_args.model_path) / \\\"adapter_config.json\\\"\\n    if adapter_config_path.exists():\\n        from peft import PeftModel\\n\\n        with open(adapter_config_path) as f:\\n            acfg = json.load(f)\\n        base_name = acfg.get(\\\"base_model_name_or_path\\\", model_args.model_name_or_path)\\n        model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(base_name, **model_kwargs)\\n        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, eval_args.model_path)\\n        model = model.merge_and_unload()\\n    else:\\n        model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(eval_args.model_path, **model_kwargs)\\n\\n    device = \\\"cuda\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\"cpu\\\"\\n    model.to(device).eval()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": Let me also check if there's an output file being written yet. And let me wait a few more minutes - the 24B model on a single GPU processing 985 samples will take a while.

Actually, I should check if the evaluate.py has a `PYTHONUNBUFFERED` issue. Since we used nohup, Python buffers all stdout. The progress lines are short enough that they won't fill the 8KB buffer for a long time (each line is ~50 bytes, so we'd need ~160 lines to fill the buffer).


Since the progress prints every 50 samples, we'd need 8000 samples to fill the buffer, which means I won't see any output until the script finishes - that's just how buffering works. I'll just keep checking back periodically.
